Ken Hagan

Hillsborough County Commission

The record covers 8,105 decisions of the Hillsborough County Commission while Ken Hagan was a member, from January 2014 to September 2026. Hagan voted no 269 times and didn't vote 1,165 times.

These are counts from the county's official vote records. They don't say why anyone voted the way they did. A no vote can mean many things, so open the official record to read more.

  • voted yes
  • voted no
  • didn't vote
  • this member

  5. May 8, 2025

    Change to the Urban Service Area (where the county plans water and sewer)

    HC/CPA 25-04 Comprehensive Plan Map Amendment – Lutz Lake Fern Road - Proposal to expand the Urban Service Area (USA) boundary to incorporate 295.6 acres, more or less

    • Size: about 295.6 acres
    What does “Change to the future land-use map” mean?

    The county's long-range plan has a map saying how each area may be used in the future and how many homes per acre are allowed at most. This changes that map for one area. A rezoning usually comes after. Source. More

    What does “Urban Service Area” mean?

    The boundary where the county plans to provide city-style services like water and sewer. The county aims to put 80% of new growth inside it. Moving the line lets land outside it ask for more homes or businesses. Source. More

    Official wording

    HC/CPA 25-04 Comprehensive Plan Map Amendment – Lutz Lake Fern Road - Proposal to expand the Urban Service Area (USA) boundary to incorporate 295.6 acres, more or less. Motioned to transmit HC/CPA 25- 04.

    Passed 6 to 1. Hagan voted no

    Official record: the Clerk's 2025 voting record, page 24. Look for roll call (RCS) 4886.

    What does “Urban Service Area” mean?

  118. February 5, 2020

    Approved the routine items together (consent agenda)

    Consent Agenda

    What does “Consent agenda” mean?

    A group of routine items approved together in one vote, to save time. Any commissioner can pull an item out to discuss it separately. More

    What does “Change to the wording of the long-range plan” mean?

    What does “7-OH (a concentrated kratom compound)” mean?

    7-hydroxymitragynine, or 7-OH, occurs in trace amounts in kratom, a Southeast Asian plant. Products with 7-OH added or concentrated, such as tablets, gummies and drink shots, are strong opioids. The FDA has approved none of them and warns they can cause serious harm. Natural kratom leaf is a separate product. Source. More

    Official wording

Decisions that mention Brandon or Lutz

Items whose official wording names Brandon or Lutz: 81. Other decisions can affect these areas too without naming them.

  1. July 21, 2026

    County gives up a public right-of-way or easement

    Change to the Urban Service Area (where the county plans water and sewer)

    HC/CPA 25-04 Comprehensive Plan Map Amendment – Lutz Lake Fern Road - Proposal to expand the Urban Service Area boundary to incorporate 295.6 acres, more or less

    • Size: about 295.6 acres
    What does “Change to the future land-use map” mean?

    The county's long-range plan has a map saying how each area may be used in the future and how many homes per acre are allowed at most. This changes that map for one area. A rezoning usually comes after. Source. More

    What does “Urban Service Area” mean?

    The boundary where the county plans to provide city-style services like water and sewer. The county aims to put 80% of new growth inside it. Moving the line lets land outside it ask for more homes or businesses. Source. More

  55. August 6, 2015

    Change to the Urban Service Area (where the county plans water and sewer)

    Consideration of ordinance - Adoption of CPA 14-10-4-M - Future Land Use Map Change

    What does “Change to the future land-use map” mean?

    The county's long-range plan has a map saying how each area may be used in the future and how many homes per acre are allowed at most. This changes that map for one area. A rezoning usually comes after. Source. More

    What does “Urban Service Area” mean?

    The boundary where the county plans to provide city-style services like water and sewer. The county aims to put 80% of new growth inside it. Moving the line lets land outside it ask for more homes or businesses. Source. More

    Official wording

  3. September 24, 2026

    Property tax rates (millage)

    Adoption of Millage Rates for FY 2027

    What does “Property tax rate (millage)” mean?

    Florida property tax rates are set in “mills.” One mill is $1 of tax for every $1,000 of a property's taxable value. A home with $200,000 of taxable value pays $200 per mill. Source. More

What these words mean

County Commission
The elected board that runs county government. In places without a city government of their own, like Brandon and Lutz, it makes the local decisions: zoning, roads, parks, the budget and county taxes.
Motion
A formal proposal by one commissioner for what the board should do. “Moved approval” means a commissioner proposed approving the item. The board then votes on it.
Consent agenda
A group of routine items approved together in one vote, to save time.
Rezoning
Changing the rules for what can be built on a piece of land. Codes like PRS, RZ and MM are the county's case numbers for these requests.
Long-range land plan
The county's Comprehensive Plan: a map and rules for how land can be used in the future. Changing it usually comes before a rezoning.
Vacating petition
A request for the county to give up a public road, alley or easement it no longer needs.
Didn't vote
The member was recorded as absent, excused, recused or not voting. The Hillsborough Clerk marks this “X” (and rarely “A”) without saying which.

Votes from the Hillsborough County Clerk's BOCC Master Voting Record (2014 on), which the Clerk updates about monthly, so the newest meetings can be a few weeks behind. Names are printed by surname in the record; we match them to full names by date.

Open Civic Web is a free, nonpartisan public project. Every night it collects public records from official government websites and keeps a fingerprinted copy, so anyone can check what was decided and whether a record later changed. Updated November 14, 2026 at 10:33 AM Eastern.
